<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the diameter of this two pence coin, in centimetres?

![the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-1/section-1-question-33-subquestion-1-00.png](/assets/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-1/section-1-question-33-subquestion-1-00.png "the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-1/section-1-question-33-subquestion-1-00.png")

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Diameter}   &= \text{Difference of two readings} \\\\
                  &= 40.3 - 10.7 \\\\
                  &= 29.6 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$29.6 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Diameter}   &= \text{Difference of two readings} \\\\
                  &= 40.3 - 10.7 \\\\
                  &= 29.6 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$29.6 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class='papername'>

<div class='question question'>

This shape is made from $9$ identical squares.

![the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-2/section-1-question-31-00.png](/assets/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-2/section-1-question-31-00.png "the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2008--group-2/section-1-question-31-00.png")

The area of the shape is $144 \ \text{cm}^2$.

What is the perimeter of the shape?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$


ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$


Let the side of the square $= s$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
9 \times s ^ 2  &= 144 \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {144} {9} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {16 \times 9} {9} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= 16 \\\\
s               &= \sqrt {16} \\\\
s               &= 4 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

*TODO: Add a description on counting all the sides of the shape which makes the perimeter*.

Let's find the perimeter of the shape.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter} &= 20 \times 4 \\\\
                 &= 80 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$80 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_square_expanded
<emptyline>
rtq_abbr_katex_formula_expanded_perimeter_square
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {144} {9} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {16 \times 9} {9} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
s ^ 2           &= 16 \\\\
s               &= \sqrt {16} \\\\
s               &= 4 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
*TODO: Add a description on counting all the sides of the shape which makes the perimeter*.
<emptyline>
Let's find the perimeter of the shape.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter} &= 20 \times 4 \\\\
                 &= 80 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$80 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What is the area of this shape?

![the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2009--group-1/section-1-question-20-00.png](/assets/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2009--group-1/section-1-question-20-00.png "the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2009--group-1/section-1-question-20-00.png")

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


*TODO:: Show the diagram divided into rectangle and triangle with the calculations for the parts of the shape which do not have measurements shown.*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As per the diagram, the shape can be divided into a triangle and a rectangle.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area}     &= \text{Area of the triangle} + \text{Area of the rectangle} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 4 \times 4 + 2 \times 4 \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 4} {2} + 8 \\\\
                &= \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 4} {2} + 8 \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 2 \times 4} {\cancel {2}} + 8 \\\\
                &= 2 \times 4 + 8 \\\\
                &= 8 + 8 \\\\
                &= 16 \ \text{units}^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16 \ \text{units}^2$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<emptyline>

                &= \dfrac {4 \times 4} {2} + 8 \\\\
                &= \dfrac {2 \times 2 \times 4} {2} + 8 \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 2 \times 4} {\cancel {2}} + 8 \\\\
                &= 2 \times 4 + 8 \\\\
                &= 8 + 8 \\\\
                &= 16 \ \text{units}^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<li class='questionContainer'>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What are the values of $\text{x}$ and $\text{y}?$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 + x + 2           &= 8 \\\\
x + 6               &= 8 \\\\
x                   &= 8 - 6 \\\\
x                   &= 2 \ \text {cm} \\\\
y + (6 - 2 - 2) + y &= 14 \\\\
2y + 2              &= 14 \\\\
2y                  &= 14 - 2 \\\\
2y                  &= 12 \\\\
y                   &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
y                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
y                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 6} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
y                   &= 6 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$x = 2 \ \text {cm} \ , y = 6 \ \text {cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the diagram,

y                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 6} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
y                   &= 6 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

                        &= 52 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$
